🤖 RAG ANSWER (65.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program, first ensure you meet the GPA
   requirements: a minimum of 2.20 for undergraduate students and 2.5 for
   graduate students. Obtain an acceptance letter from a host organization
   for an internship lasting at least 2 months, which must align with your
   degree program’s field. Submit your application through the Online
   Application System by following the procedures outlined in the program
   guidelines, including uploading your CV, transcript, and the acceptance
   letter to the Internship Office. Applications are evaluated based on
   Erasmus+ scores, and the Student Selection Committee approves the final
   list in mid-April. Before departure, the Erasmus+ Internship Mobility
   Agreement must be signed by the host organization. Language scores for
   undergraduates are determined using ENG 101-102 grades or SPS/AL course
   grades via the Council of Higher Education’s equivalence table.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (91.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliği hibeleri, öğrencinin başarı puanına göre
   sıralanarak dağıtılmaktadır. Hibelerin miktarı ve ödeme şekli, Ulusal
   Ajans tarafından her yıl ülke bazında belirlenir ve üniversitelere
   bildirilir. Öğrencilerin hibe alabilmesi için başvuruları, Kariyer
   Geliştirme ve Staj Ofisi tarafından oluşturulan Erasmus+ Puan sırasına
   göre değerlendirilir. Bu puan, dil sınav puanının %50’si ve Genel Not
   Ortalaması’nın (GNO) %50’si alınarak hesaplanır (bu kural lisansüstü
   öğrenciler için geçerlidir). Hibeler, öğrenci listesi Öğrenci Seçim
   Komitesi onayından sonra toplam hibe miktarı doğrultusunda öğrencilere
   sırayla verilir. Ulusal Ajans tarafından belirlenen aylık hibe miktarı,
   hareketliliğin süresiyle çarpılarak toplam tutar belirlenir; tamamlanmayan
   aylar için ise gün sayısı üzerinden hesap yapılır. Öğrenci, hibe
   anlaşmasının imzalanması ve ilk Öğrenim Anlaşması’nın tamamlanması
   şartıyla ödeme alabilir.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (63.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) is prepared, signed, and submitted by the
   selected student within the timeframe announced by the university, with
   the approval of the exchange academic advisor. The advisor determines the
   equivalent credits, course types, and ECTS values for the selected courses
   at the partner institution. The student must ensure that the courses taken
   abroad fulfill SU graduation requirements and inform the advisor of any
   changes before completing the term. The signed LA must also be approved by
   the partner institution and submitted to the International Relations
   Office (IRO). For graduate students, the LA is prepared online in
   consultation with the advisor and submitted to IRO within the specified
   timeline. After signatures are obtained, the IRO forwards the LA to the
   Faculty Administrative Officer for approval. Students must also submit the
   signed LA, transcript, and Certificate of Attendance upon returning from
   the exchange program.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (74.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary objection process at Sabancı University allows students
   to object to sanctions within **15 days** of notification by submitting a
   written petition to the University Administrative Board. The board
   examines the objection and either accepts or rejects the sanction within
   15 days. If rejected, the disciplinary board or supervisor reviews the
   objection considering the rejection grounds. For housing-related removal
   decisions, objections must be submitted within **5 working days** of
   notification, and the Disciplinary Board decides within **7 working
   days**. If a student appeals to the administrative jurisdiction and a stay
   of execution is granted, the Student Records (SR) notifies the
   disciplinary supervisor and relevant units in **1 day** upon receiving the
   court order. Notifications to units about disciplinary punishments are
   finalized within **3 days** after objection resolution if an objection is
   filed. Expelled students’ suspension periods are not counted toward their
   maximum study period, and suspended students must pay tuition for the
   semester they are suspended.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Disiplin suçlarında zamanaşımı süreleri ceza türüne göre değişir:  -
   **Uyarma, kınama veya 1 hafta ile 1 ay arası uzaklaştırma cezaları** için,
   fiilin soruşturmaya yetkili amirce öğrenildiği tarihten itibaren **1 ay**
   içinde soruşturulmaya başlanmazsa zamanaşımı olur.  - **1 veya 2 yarıyıl
   uzaklaştırma veya yükseköğretim kurumundan çıkarma cezaları** için, aynı
   tarihten itibaren **3 ay** içinde soruşturulmaya başlanmazsa zamanaşımı
   olur.  - **Genel disiplin suçları** için, fiilin işlendiği tarihten
   itibaren **2 yıl** içinde ceza verilmezse zamanaşımı olur. Ancak adli
   yargı kararı gerektiren durumlarda, zamanaşımı süresi adli kararın
   kesinleştiği tarihten başlar.  - **Cinsel taciz suçları** için, eylemin
   işlendiği tarihten itibaren **2 yıl** içinde soruşturma tamamlanmalı ve
   karara bağlanmalıdır.   Bu süreler, ceza türüne ve suç niteliğine göre
   belirlenmiştir.

   Burs devam şartları program ve öğrenci türüne göre farklılık gösterir:  1.
   **Lisans Bursları**: Üniversiteye ilk girişte kazanılan burslar, normal
   öğrenim süresi boyunca devam eder. Mezun olma koşullarını yerine
   getiremeyen öğrencilerin burs süresi en fazla iki dönem uzatılabilir. Burs
   süresi, normal öğrenim süresini aşarsa otomatik olarak sona erer.  2.
   **Lisansüstü Bursları**:   - **Yüksek Lisans**: Maksimum 10 ay (Eylül-
   Haziran), **Doktora**: 12 ay (Eylül-Ağustos) süreyle aylık nakit ödeme
   yapılır.   - Burs devamlılığı her dönem başında Enstitü Yönetim Kurulu
   kararı ile değerlendirilir. Güz dönemi için 15 Ağustos, Bahar dönemi için
   15 Ocak itibariyle değerlendirme yapılır.  3. **İzinli Dönemler**: Güz
   döneminde izin alan öğrencilerin bursu o dönem için kesilir, Bahar dönemi
   ders kaydı yapıldığında tekrar aktif hale gelir.  4. **Kayıt Silinmesi**:
   Üniversite kaydı silinen öğrencilerin bursları tamamen sona erer.  5.
   **Disiplin Cezaları**: Disiplin cezası alan öğrencilerin burs durumu
   normal öğrenim süresi içinde değerlendirilir (detaylı şartlar
   belirtilmemiş).   Bu koşullar, lisans ve lisansüstü öğrenciler için ayrı
   ayrı tanımlanmıştır.

------------------------------------------------------------
   The Sabancı University Main Endowment Scholarship Fund is a financial aid
   initiative designed to support undergraduate and graduate students who
   demonstrate academic success and financial need. Donations to the fund
   come from SU employees, alumni, students, and other individuals or
   institutions, with contributions from employees typically deducted from
   their monthly gross salary at a self-determined rate. The fund’s
   development is managed by the Institutional Partnerships and Alumni
   Relations (IPAR) unit, and its principles are approved by the Board of
   Trustees. Additionally, international donations from U.S.-based
   contributors can be channeled through the "Friends of Sabancı University
   Fund" under the Turkish Philanthropic Fund to leverage U.S. tax
   incentives.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (59.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The graduate program opening process at Sabancı University requires the
   Graduate School Board to prepare a proposal with details (courses,
   credits, program purpose) in YOKSIS, obtain the UEC’s opinion under the
   VP-E’s coordination, secure Academic Council (AC) approval, and gain Board
   of Trustees and YOK approval. Education begins after YOK’s approval. For
   closure, the Graduate School Board submits justifications to the AC for
   approval, followed by Board of Trustees and YOK approval. The SR notifies
   YOK of the closure decision within 3 business days of the Board of
   Trustees’ approval and removes the program from the web within 1 week of
   YOK’s approval. Updates to existing programs (e.g., course requirements)
   follow a separate procedure for course changes.

📊 VERIFIED FACTS (From Knowledge Graph):

[Gpa Calculation]
  • Requirement: alınan tüm dersler GNO hesaplarında kullanılır (lisans)
  • Requirement: all digits after comma are taken into consideration
  • Requirement: last grade for a repeated course is taken into account
  • Requirement: tekrar edilen derslerden alınan en son not dikkate alınır (lisans)
  • Credits: total SU credits of the courses taken in that term (lisansustu)

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

  • Sabancı University Grad...
================
[Generation] Stripped document codes from answer.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (65.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   At Sabancı University, GPA is calculated differently for undergraduate and
   graduate programs. For **undergraduate students**, the GPA is determined
   by dividing the total SU credit points (calculated by multiplying each
   course's SU credit by its coefficient) by the total SU credits of all
   courses taken. The Cumulative GPA (CGPA) includes all courses from
   enrollment onward. Repeated courses use the last grade received, and all
   decimal digits are considered in academic rankings.   For **graduate
   students**, the GPA is calculated per term by dividing the total credit
   points of courses taken in that term by the total SU credits for those
   courses. The CGPA similarly aggregates all courses from enrollment.
   Repeated courses also use the last grade, and academic rankings consider
   all decimal digits. Both programs use SU credits as defined by the
   National Credit System.
------------------------------------------------------------

   elde edemeyen öğrencilere e-posta ile bilgi verilir. Geç başvurular için
   "Geç Mezuniyet Başvuru Formu" kullanılabilir.   Güz dönemi mezuniyet
   başvuruları genellikle Kasım/Aralık, bahar dönemi başvuruları Ekim ayında

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (55.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Leave of Absence process requires students to apply within specific
   deadlines and pay a percentage of tuition fees. Undergraduate students
   must apply by the **last business day of the 4th week** (paying **25%** of
   the semester/year fee) or by the **7th week** (paying **50%**). Graduate
   students follow similar deadlines but with approval from the Graduate
   School Administrative Board. Reasons for leave include health, financial,
   family, or academic issues, requiring supporting documentation.
   Conscripted or legally affected students must apply within **2 months** of
   the situation. For graduates, returning from leave involves submitting a
   petition to the Graduate School Directorate, with the leave fee deducted
   from the tuition upon approval. Undergraduate approvals require the School
   of Languages or Faculty Administrative Board, while graduate approvals
   involve the relevant Graduate School Board.
------------------------------------------------------------

   değerlendirilir ve sonuçlar, ders kayıtları başlamadan önce duyurulur.

   başlamalıdır.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (54.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include
   warnings, reprimands, temporary suspensions (1 week to 1 month or 1–2
   semesters), and dismissal. For warnings, reprimands, or short-term
   suspensions (1 week to 1 month), disciplinary proceedings must begin
   within 1 month of the offense; for longer suspensions or dismissal,
   proceedings must start within 3 months. Students suspended for at least
   one semester must pay tuition for the suspended semester and are
   prohibited from campus access or using university facilities during the
   suspension period. Dormitory-specific penalties include warnings,
   reprimands, or removal from student residences, with appeals allowed
   within 5 working days. Repeated disciplinary offenses result in a penalty
   one grade heavier than the initial sanction, though dismissal cannot be
   imposed for repeated violations. Scholarship continuation for sanctioned
   students is evaluated under separate guidelines.
------------------------------------------------------------

[Graduation Audit]

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (81.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The graduation audit process begins when students apply through the
   Student Resources (SR) via SIS within the academic calendar's specified
   dates. Students must meet requirements such as completing required
   courses, credits, and grade point averages, which are monitored semester-
   by-semester by both the student and their institute. For graduate programs
   with thesis, additional documentation (e.g., thesis proposal, defense
   records) is verified by the student, advisor, and institute. If eligible,
   the institute submits a graduation list to SR with Graduate School
   Director approval; students not registered due to disciplinary actions or
   leave cannot apply. Prospective graduates who fail the audit must reapply
   in the next semester. Diplomas are issued after final approval, with
   specific signing protocols for undergraduate and postgraduate diplomas.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (56.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Persons who find lost items on campus should report them by calling
   extension 3565 or delivering them to the Security Office in Building D2.
   Security officers prepare a two-copy Found Item Return Form, which is
   signed by both the finder and the officer. The original form remains at
   the Security Unit, while the second copy is given to the finder. Found
   items are numbered and categorized as valuable (e.g., electronics, money,
   precious metals) or non-valuable, with each item placed in a separate
   labeled bag. If a student loses an item on campus, they must notify the
   Security Unit verbally and in writing (via
   https://suticket.sabanciuniv.edu) and provide a police report for theft or
   loss off-campus. The Security Directorate oversees the process, including
   storage and potential return or disposal.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University provides several shuttle services:  1. **University
   Shuttle Services** for all campus arrivals/departures, operating on routes
   and hours set by Transportation Services (TS), requiring registration via
   SUCard for reserved routes.  2. **Shift and Collection Services** for
   shift workers, following announced hours and routes.  3. **Private Student
   Shuttles** to coordinate student transportation with external companies.
   4. **On-Campus Ring Service** for intra-campus transportation at scheduled
   times and routes.  5. **Special Service Requests** for shuttle services
   during artistic, academic, or social events.  6. **Personnel Shuttles**
   for employees, interns, and subcontractors (not student-specific).
   Requests for route changes or special services are submitted via SUTicket
   under "SER - Transportation/Ulaşım."

🤖 RAG ANSWER (59.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services at Sabancı University are arranged
   through the Food and Beverage Services (FBS) unit. Units must submit the
   "Special Event and Catering Request Form" via SUForm **30 days before the
   event** to ensure timely processing. FBS prepares preliminary offers,
   including menus and specifications, based on the form details. The
   Procurement and Operations Unit (POP) then handles company selection and
   procurement requests, transmitting the finalized proposal to the
   requesting unit and FBS. Event execution involves coordination between
   FBS, the selected company, and the requesting unit, with FBS overseeing
   operations and follow-up. Units are responsible for addressing any
   deficiencies caused by late form submissions, and advance procurement
   requests for future dates are not permitted.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Kampüs servis hizmetleri, Sabancı Üniversitesi tarafından belirlenen
   güzergah ve saatlere göre düzenlenir. Üniversite Shuttle Servisleri,
   ilgili yasalara uygun olarak kampüs ile şehir arasında düzenli seferlerle
   hizmet verir. Kampüs İçi Ring Servisi, iç web sayfasında yayınlanan
   saatlere göre belirlenen güzergâhta (Ana Kapı > Spor Merkezi > DO > YBF
   Önü > Yurtlar > SUNUM > Shuttle Alanı > Ana Kapı) hafta içi ve hafta sonu
   farklı saatlerde sefer yapar. Özel öğrenci servisleri, öğrenci ile firma
   arasındaki koordinasyonu sağlayacak şekilde yüklenici firmalar tarafından
   yürütülür. Üniversite birimleri veya fakülteler, sanatsal, akademik veya
   sosyal etkinlikler için ücretli özel servis talepleri de yapabilir. Yeni
   işe başlayan çalışanlar mevcut güzergâh üzerinde yakın bir duraktan
   alınır; güzergâh değişiklikleri karayolları yasaları ve üniversite
   prosedürlerine göre UH kararı ile uygulanır.

------------------------------------------------------------
   To be eligible for the Rectorate Research Fund, applicants must be full-
   time faculty members who have completed at least six months at Sabancı
   University and started their activities within the last two years. They
   must ensure their proposed projects align with their existing or ongoing
   research activities. By the project’s end, they are required to submit at
   least two new documented external grant applications (e.g., to ERC,
   Horizon Europe, TÜBİTAK) or secure one guaranteed external financing.
   Additionally, they must publish high-impact scientific articles. The
   maximum project duration is 36 months, with theoretical research budgets
   capped at 300,000 TL and experimental research (defined as projects where
   laboratory equipment, consumables, or experimental field studies exceed
   30% of the total budget) at 600,000 TL. Faculty members are permitted to
   apply only once to this fund.
